Regular Business Meeting 
The Citizens Budget Advisory Committee met in a regular meeting that was called to order 
at 7:31 A.M. 
 
Present: Loretta Huff (Chair), Sandy Curran (Vice Chair), Chris Davell, Marv DeSelm, 
Dolly Kelly, Peter Luptovic, Dolly Kelly, Karen Oxman, Bethany Thomas, Ross Fraser, Saoirse 
Charis-Graves, Jim Dale, Kathy Smith 
 
Absent: LouAnne Dale, Jamie Engesser, Bill Litz, Kasia Zak, Cynthia Parker-Kirby 
 
Staff:  Joe Allaire – Director of Finance, Carly Lorentz – Interim City Manager 
 
Roll Call occurred @7:33 AM 
 
Approval of the Agenda 
The agenda was amended @7:34 AM to add City Manager update and defer the bylaws 
discussion to October. The agenda was unanimously approved @7:34 AM 
 
Approval of August 19th, 2026, Regular Meeting Minutes 
The minutes from the August 19th, 2026, meeting were unanimously approved @7:35 AM 
 
Sales tax report discussed @7:38 AM 
 
Motion to make recommendation @7:44 AM whereas we are $100,000+ over on our 
marijuana tax, motion to make recommendation to give more money for rent assistance 
and feeding the hungry 
 
Discussion starts @7:46 AM 
 
Motion amended @7:53 AM $105,000 be given as appropriate to the needy through the 
marijuana fund for food insecurity and housing insecurity   
 
Motion passes @7:57 AM 
 
Audit Report discussed @7:59 AM  
 
 
 
Audit questions @8:02 AM 
 
ACFR questions end @8:13 AM 
 
Budget discussion starts @8:16 AM 
 
Noted to add back fund balance line at the bottom of the fund pages @8:51 AM 
 
Water rate questions @8:53 AM 
 
Budget questions end @9:00 AM 
 
Questions about Visit Golden @9:01 AM  
 
Carly starts updating the group @9:07 AM

Carly addresses Visit Golden @9:08 AM 
 
Ulysses discussed @9:16 AM

Citizens Budget Advisory Committee (CBAC) 
2026 Calendar and Agenda
Updated: October 1, 2026 
All meetings are held at 7:30 A.M. in the City Council Chambers at Golden City Hall, unless otherwise 
noted. Agendas will be posted on the City’s website at 
https://www.cityofgolden.gov/government/public_meetings.php.
